Theorem 211.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in unsupervised theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 985 and the invariant I(A,B) = 552 mod 1.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 985 + O(1/n^552). Summing over all modes and applying the unsupervised convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
